## Mandatory Agent Rule

For any CAS Harness project work, future AI agents and LLMs must:

1. Read this file before giving project advice, planning work, or changing code.
2. Update this file before the final response when statuses, blockers, next steps, or production priorities change.
3. Preserve claim-status language exactly: `ACCEPT`, `FLAG_FOR_HUMAN_REVIEW`, `LICENSED`, `NOT LICENSED`, `OPEN`, `unavailable`, `present`.
4. Separate research results from product readiness. A strong research result is not automatically a production feature.
5. If the user explicitly says `READ ONLY`, do not edit this file. Instead, say what update should be made.

Plain-English mandate: this file is the project dashboard. Check the dashboard before talking, and update the dashboard before leaving.

and clears the remembered last-good base when the owner changes or resets the configured API base. The shared client
only cools down transport/timeout failures and `API 404` route-miss responses; normal application errors do not

2026-07-01 CAS Forex funding model clarified for live-account readiness.
The owner asked where live trading capital is loaded and whether funding belongs in CAS Forex, the broker
website, or MT4/MT5. The product boundary remains: funding the live trading account is handled through the
selected broker's client portal/account area, while CAS Forex provides the broker portal handoff and then syncs
the broker account snapshot after funding so balance, equity, margin, and account ownership can drive risk
sizing, readiness checks, and AI trade control. MT4/MT5 is the trade terminal/bridge, not the primary place to
enter raw bank/card funding details. CAS Forex should continue to avoid raw bank/card storage and direct custody
while making the funding route, post-funding sync, and owner authority obvious in Proof and Settings. This does
not alter owner `ACCEPT`, broker credentials/config, edge authorization, MT4/MT5 dispatch, live trading,
deposits, withdrawals, bank/card handling, payout automation, or funding custody.

2026-07-01 CAS Forex Dashboard/Operator runtime overlay now reuses the hot WASTE runner cache.
Live profiling after the Dashboard fast-packet split showed `/api/forex/dashboard/fast`
and `/api/forex/waste-loop/stage` were already small and fast, but the Operator
Briefing and autonomous-loop health overlays still had a path that could rebuild
WASTE runner status directly while rendering the Dashboard. `api/routes/autonomous_loop.py`
now reads the prewarmed `waste_runner_status_fast` route-cache snapshot first and
starts a background refresh on cache miss instead of doing an inline WASTE status
build for every overlay. This lets Operator Briefing and autonomous-loop health

2026-07-01 CAS Forex owner live-authority boundary reinforced.
The runtime live gate now states the owner-control hierarchy directly:
CAS Forex metrics, AI agents, paper-proof day counts, 12-month / multi-symbol
evidence, win-rate estimates, and profit pace are advisory only and cannot
authorize or veto live trading. Runtime authorization is controlled by the
owner's switches / `ACCEPT` buttons plus technical requirements that must exist
at action time, such as broker credentials, provider config, edge
authorization, bridge checks, and risk checks. `runtime_gates` now returns this
`owner_authority_boundary` and an `advisory_only_inputs` list in its report, and

2026-07-01 CAS Forex Decision History cold-read slimming completed.
The Decision History page was the slowest route in the full-app smoke
(`decision-history` took about `7.8 s` in the broad browser pass). Root cause:
`/api/forex/agent-decision/history` sorted cycle-report paths but then parsed
every saved cycle report before slicing to the requested limit. The route now
passes `limit` into the disk loader, and the default loader slices newest
paths before JSON parsing, so `limit=10` reads only the newest 10 cycle
reports rather than all 55 currently on disk. Regression coverage now proves
the default loader reads only the requested newest files. Verification:
`python -m py_compile api\routes\agent_decision_history.py
tests\test_agent_decision_history_route.py` passed; `python -m pytest
tests\test_agent_decision_history_route.py -q` passed (`7 passed`, two FastAPI
deprecation warnings); the API was restarted on `127.0.0.1:8765` with
`C:\Python314\python.exe -m uvicorn api.server:app --host 0.0.0.0 --port
8765`; `/api/forex/health` returned 200; warm direct timings after restart
were roughly `80-221 ms` for `limit=10`, `105-123 ms` for `limit=25`, and
`130-219 ms` for `limit=50`; browser smoke on
`/?page=decision-history` reached content-ready state in `289 ms` after
DOMContentLoaded, total navigation `1762 ms`, with no disabled buttons, no
`API unreachable`, no fixture fallback, no visible `NOT LICENSED`, visible
WASTE live state, and no console errors. Safety boundary unchanged: read-only
history/explanation work only; no live orders, deposits, withdrawals, raw

authorized.

`/api/forex/health`, and `/health` is excluded from synchronous request tracing

tests\test_settings_api_surface.py` passed; `python -m pytest

deprecation warnings); `git diff --check` passed for the touched API/test files;

`/api/forex/health` returning 200, dashboard shell/fast returning 200, and
`/api/forex/waste-loop/stage` still returning `running: true`,
`stage: no_trade`, `mode: PAPER`. Safety boundary unchanged: health/liveness only, no
live orders, deposits, withdrawals, bank/card movement, payouts, broker
`ACCEPT`, or `LICENSED` status.

2026-07-01 CAS Forex WASTE heartbeat split-brain repair completed. Live smoke
found the WASTE runner fast route truthfully reporting `running: true`,
`current_stage: no_trade`, and `mode: PAPER`, while the activity indicator's
`/api/forex/waste-loop/stage` route could still return `running: false`,
`stage: idle` after an API restart. Root cause: the stage route accepted the
fresh durable supervisor lease, then incorrectly vetoed it when the optional
worker registry row was unconfigured (`configured: false`, `running: false`).
`api.routes.loop_stage._runner_is_live(...)` now only lets an external-worker
row veto the heartbeat when that row is explicitly configured and reports not
running. The frontend indicator also falls back from `/waste-loop/stage` to the
fast WASTE status route before checking dispatch-loop status, so the header
stays connected to the durable runner even during API memory resets. Verification:
`python -m py_compile api\routes\loop_stage.py tests\test_loop_heartbeat.py`
passed; `python -m pytest tests\test_loop_heartbeat.py -q` passed (`6 passed`);
`python -m pytest tests\test_loop_heartbeat.py tests\test_settings_api_surface.py
-q` passed (`30 passed`); UI `npm run build` passed; API was restarted on PID
`66612`; live smoke showed `/api/forex/waste-loop/stage` returning
`running: true`, `stage: no_trade`, `source: waste_runner_status`; browser smoke
on `/?page=proof`, `/?page=pcba`, `/?page=trade`, and `/?page=dashboard` showed
the activity indicator as `LIVE`, `No Trade`, no `API unreachable`, no fixture
fallback, and no console errors. Safety boundary unchanged: observational
heartbeat only, no live orders, deposits, withdrawals, bank/card movement,
payouts, broker `ACCEPT`, or `LICENSED` status.

smoke showed the isolated WASTE runner was genuinely running in `PAPER`, while
the paper sample collector's latest evidence packet could still mark

2026-07-01 CAS Forex Trade cockpit actionability/latency repair completed.
After the Trade-page completion lane landed, runtime sampling exposed a startup
latency spike where even `/api/forex/health` could sit behind expensive gate
report rebuilding during cache warmup. `cas_forex.live_enable_control` now
caches default owner authorization/live-enable reports for a short runtime TTL
and invalidates that cache immediately whenever the owner saves switches,
provider settings, clears overrides, or changes the override store. This keeps
the cockpit responsive during normal polling while preserving immediate owner
button feedback after `SAVE PROVIDER / DESTINATION` or `TURN OWNER SWITCHES ON`.
Regression coverage was added in `tests/test_live_enable_control.py` to prove a
default authorization report is reused and then invalidated by an owner update.
Runtime was reloaded on API PID `43908`; owner switch overrides are still ON in
`cas-forex/runtime/live-enable-overrides.json`, while payment provider,
provider API key, and payout destination remain empty because the owner still
has to supply provider-token/destination data. Focused verification:
`python -m py_compile cas-forex/cas_forex/live_enable_control.py
cas-forex/api/routes/live_enable.py` passed; `python -m pytest
tests/test_live_enable_control.py tests/test_live_money_progression_packet.py

2026-06-30 CAS Forex WASTE paper probe scanner implemented. The paper-only
sample collector no longer has to sit on one configured probe lane while waiting
for a PASS-token sample. `waste_paper_sample_collector.py` now supports a
bounded read-only WASTE real-candle scan over strategy-ledger-eligible
`ACTIVE_RESEARCH_LEAD` symbols/timeframes. The scanner is exposed through
`scan_candidates` and `max_scan_candidates` on the paper sample collector
run-once and scheduler routes, and the scheduler now persists those settings in
its owner-visible config. If no explicit timeframe is supplied, the scanner uses
each active lead's preferred timeframes; if a PASS appears, it stops on that

2026-06-30 CAS Forex WASTE future PASS-token paper execution sink implemented.
`waste_runner.py` now gives the timed WASTE runner a default PASS-only paper/demo
dispatch sink. When WASTE reaches EXECUTE from a fresh edge-gate PASS, it selects
the owner-configured bridge through `broker_control.select_bridge`, builds the
exact `waste-{decision_ref}` order, sends it through the normal
`order_dispatch.dispatch_intent` chokepoint, and records the result in
`cas-forex/runtime/order-attempts.json` with source `waste_runner`. This does not
create owner `ACCEPT`, broker `ACCEPT`, live authority, deposits, withdrawals,
bank/card movement, or payout actions. The dispatch still re-checks the edge
authorization and risk/live gates before any bridge can be touched. The runner
status now exposes `paper_execution_sink` so the owner can see that the sink is
configured and where its authority boundary is. A PASS-token timestamp bug was
also fixed: the runner now preserves the edge gate's original `evaluated_at` when
registering the PASS, so HMAC verification at the dispatch chokepoint can
actually validate the token. `waste_cycle_audit.py` now serializes dispatch
results as structured JSON so future executed PASS cycles can show broker ticket
and audit status in the durable WASTE cycle record. Focused tests prove a future
PASS reaches the default paper sink, writes a finalized non-real order attempt,
and remains fail-closed without PASS. Existing fake-real bridge tests were moved

2026-06-30 CAS Forex auto-halt circuit breakers implemented. Added
`cas-forex\cas_forex\auto_halt.py`, a durable live-money safety latch stored in
`runtime\auto-halt-state.json`. The evaluator checks live account drawdown,
consecutive closed-trade losses, live-vs-paper realized-P/L divergence, and
broker-truth reconciliation failure. When a hard breaker trips, it persists
`AUTO_HALT_ACTIVE` with `force_paper_mode=true`; `runtime_gates.evaluate_live_trading`
now requires "auto-halt circuit breaker is CLEAR", so live trading becomes
`CURRENTLY NOT LICENSED` while the halt latch is active even if owner live flags
and broker credentials are otherwise present. Added `/api/forex/auto-halt/status`
to evaluate/report the breaker and `/api/forex/auto-halt/clear?confirm_clear=true`
to clear it only after explicit owner confirmation. Dashboard payload now
includes `autoHalt` from `api.data.auto_halt_status()`. Verification:
`tests/test_auto_halt.py`, `tests/test_auto_halt_route.py`,
`tests/test_runtime_gates.py`, `tests/test_order_dispatch.py`,

closed by the next entry. Remaining gap: continuous broker-truth reconciliation
still needs a live polling/worker path.

2026-06-30 CAS Forex MT5 live closed-history ingest path implemented. The

2026-06-29 CAS Forex verified-profit sweep accounting slice implemented. Added

no provider receipt and no money movement. Audit rows append to

2026-06-29 CAS Forex WASTE strategize-to-data wire implemented. Added
`cas-forex\cas_forex\waste_market_data.py`, which fetches candles through the
existing market-data connector, selects an owner-allowed symbol/timeframe,
builds a WASTE market state, and attaches recent causal OOS `entry_pairs` for
the same directional pattern. Each historical pair scores LONG/SHORT/NO_TRADE
using only candles available at that past decision point, stores signed
`signal_strength` (LONG positive, SHORT negative), then measures raw future
return and cost for that past window. The current candle is excluded from its
own proof. `waste_runner._default_watch` now tries this data-backed watcher and
falls back to an empty no-trade state if no feed is reachable; `_default_analyze`
and `_default_strategize` can now form a directional candidate when candles are
present. Directional candidates still do not authorize execution: without enough
or good enough OOS `entry_pairs`, the edge gate returns `NO_TRADE_*`; only a
fresh `PASS` token can reach dispatch. Verification: focused
authority/directional/data/WASTE/edge/dispatch safety suite `136 passed`; `from
api.server import app` succeeded with `152` routes. Next product action: connect
UI controls to `/api/forex/ai-control-authority`, expose WASTE data status in
the cockpit, and then build verified-profit accounting/profit-sweep controls
from broker-confirmed closed trades.

2026-06-29 CAS Forex final objective clarification. The product goal is real
money capable CAS Forex, not paper/demo as an end state. Paper/demo remains the
proof stage before live money is owner-unlocked. Final system must support live
broker trading after readiness gates pass, owner risk/daily-loss/daily-profit
cap/emergency-stop controls, broker-confirmed closed-trade net-winnings
accounting, user-set sweep percentage, tax-reserve percentage withheld before
sweep, explicit bank/payout setup and confirmation, payout audit logs, and
small-controlled-amount testing before scaling. Hard rules: never fake profit
numbers, never call demo profit real profit, never move money without explicit
user setup and confirmation, never withdraw principal by accident unless the
owner explicitly chooses that, keep emergency stop available, and log every live
trade/profit calculation/payout action. Reliability gaps to close before real
money scale: disconnect/crash recovery, continuous broker-truth reconciliation
for partial fills/requotes/stop-outs/orphaned positions, auto-halt circuit
breakers for max account drawdown/consecutive losses, live-vs-paper divergence
monitor that reverts to paper when live behavior diverges from validated
expectation, and broker withdrawal/KYC/fee/currency-conversion/ToS checks.

2026-06-29 CAS Forex AI Control Authority slice started. Implemented durable
backend control authority in `cas-forex\cas_forex\ai_control_authority.py`,
directional long/short/no-trade hypothesis scoring in
`cas-forex\cas_forex\directional_intelligence.py`, API route
`cas-forex\api\routes\ai_control_authority.py`, server registration at
`/api/forex/ai-control-authority`, WASTE runner wiring to read the durable
authority by default, and `runtime/` ignore entry for local mutable authority
state. Owner envelope now records the `$5,000` monthly target, `$250` daily
floor, `$3,500` daily cap, `2.5%` incentive rate, allowed symbols/sessions,
LONG/SHORT permissions, edge-threshold bounds, risk ceiling, concurrent-trade
ceiling, compound-unit ceiling, and fresh-PASS requirements. The AI can adjust
runtime levers only inside that owner envelope; this route does not enable live
trading or money movement. Directional intelligence can now form a LONG or
SHORT WASTE `CandidateSetup` from injected candles, but without `entry_pairs`
the edge gate still stops at `NO_TRADE_INSUFFICIENT_BARS`. This is the intended
first step: mutable intelligence can propose direction, while execution remains
PASS-gated. Verification: focused authority/directional plus existing
AI/WASTE/edge/dispatch safety suite `133 passed`; `from api.server import app`
succeeded with `152` routes. Next product action: wire a real
watcher/data source to feed candles plus recent OOS `entry_pairs` into WASTE,
then connect the UI sliders/buttons to `/api/forex/ai-control-authority`.

2026-06-29 CAS Forex prior range-scalper research assessment. The attached
prior-agent transcript was checked against the repo and is real: commit
`c2fd41f2` added `scripts\run_range_scalper_test.py`, and commit `4c7f0bc7`
added `scripts\run_gbp_scalper_validate.py`. The benefit is research/control
value, not a deployable edge. The early saved JSON at
`cas-forex\evidence_runs\range_scalper_realtest\range-scalper-realtest-report.json`
shows the bright spot: `GBPUSD_D1`, `18` trades, `94.44%` win rate,
`+1273.3` pips at `2.0` pip spread, beating the random-entry null. The later
stress-test script exists because that sample was too thin; the pasted
transcript records the longer-history result as not robust: `89` trades,
roughly breakeven, not beating null, sensitive to dropping top winners,
unstable by half-split, and fragile under parameter nudges. Product action:
do not let future agents revive the `94%` headline as a trade authorization.
Use range-scalper only as a weak WASTE watch-pattern and as a template for the
AI Control Authority's fragility checks: sample size, null outperformance,
walk-forward stability, leave-best-trades-out, parameter jitter, spread/cost
sensitivity, and cross-symbol robustness. `strategy_leads.json` still contains
older active-research language for `range_scalper_filtered_fx_d1`; this was
corrected on 2026-06-30 by adding the superseding stress-test evidence and
demoting the lead to watch-only/fragile so UI and agents do not overweight it.

2026-06-29 CAS Forex owner-governance clarification. The owner/user is the
only authority who can approve Hugosway use, real-money trading, broker
`ACCEPT`, money movement, and whether a capability is `LICENSED`. Agents must
not present their own caution as the owner's licensing decision. When an agent
reports `NOT LICENSED` / `CURRENTLY NOT LICENSED`, it means the current
recorded software gate/status is not enabled or not evidenced yet; it does not
mean the agent has authority over the owner's final decision. Product design
target: AI autonomy inside an owner-authorized envelope. CAS Harness / CAS
PCBA should grant, limit, and audit tool permissions; WASTE should use mutable
intelligence to Watch, Analyze, Strategize, Test, then Execute; the AI should
be date-aware, objective-aware, and able to adjust allowed levers such as edge
thresholds, position sizing, profit-taking, principal-lock/breakeven behavior,
and lacunarity/RPM/CJT compounding within owner-set limits. Owner objective:
target at least `$5,000` monthly profit, modeled as `$250` per trading day
across `20` forex trading days, with the incentive ledger tracking the stated
`2.5%` AI incentive on verified profit. Separate owner authorization from
evidence: owner approval can allow a lane to run, but profitability still must
be measured by receipts, costs, drawdown, null controls, and account evidence.
Next product focus: build the AI Control Authority layer that turns these
owner permissions and objectives into durable backend controls, not just UI
local state.

Important product-readiness distinction: the WASTE heartbeat can be `LIVE`,
but the default global runner still does not yet evaluate live broker candles
or propose real setups. `_default_watch` returns an unknown regime,
`_default_analyze` returns zero confidence, `_default_strategize` returns

Current edge evidence remains: no public-data lever tested so far has shown a
tradeable, cost-adjusted edge that beats null controls, including the
intraday macro test. The most money-directed next research action is to encode
the user's untested discretionary NAS30/NAS100 rule as a formal, no-lookahead,
cost-adjusted, null-guarded OOS hypothesis, then paper/demo only if it beats
null. Live trading, broker `ACCEPT`, auto-deposit, debit/card/bank movement,
and payout automation remain `NOT LICENSED` / `CURRENTLY NOT LICENSED`.

2026-06-24 (phase 2) CAS Forex MT5 process guard implemented and tested.

loop. Five worker operations: `execute_demo_order`, `get_deal_history`,
`get_account_snapshot`, `get_open_positions`, `close_position`. Integrated
into `agent_state.py` via `mt5_use_process_guard` +
`mt5_guard_timeout_seconds` params; falls through to existing direct MT5 path
when guard is off. CLI flags added to `run_agent_workflow_queue.py`. 14 new
tests in `test_mt5_process_guard.py`. Also implemented CAS Forex Bot
(`cas-forex\cas_forex\cas_forex_bot.py`): 31 capabilities across 7 action
groups, menu-driven, confirmation gates, command transparency. Entry point:
`py -3.12 scripts/run_cas_forex_bot.py`. Full test suite: `494 passed` (14
new guard tests, zero regressions). Next action: rerun scheduled MT5 demo
cycle against real Hugosway terminal with guard enabled.

2026-06-24 CAS Forex receipt-aware overseer implemented: the overseer now reads
the append-only event journal before approving same-symbol route intents. If an
AUD/CAD route already has command files waiting for terminal receipts, the next
AUD/CAD micro-scalp/hourly route is rejected as `QUEUED_AWAITING_RECEIPT`
instead of writing another MT4 command. If a future terminal receipt verifies
and reconciles to `NEXT_CYCLE_ALLOWED`, the same symbol can route again. If a
receipt mismatch produces `TERMINAL_LOCKED_FOR_SYMBOL`, the same symbol remains
locked until the mismatch is handled. Updated
`cas-forex\cas_forex\agent_overseer.py`,
`cas-forex\cas_forex\agent_state.py`,
`cas-forex\cas_forex\agent_message_bus.py`,
`cas-forex\ui\src\data\fixtures.ts`, and added

regressions. Fixed API-surface tests so future verification uses temp evidence

2026-06-23 CAS Forex provider-backed movement snapshot path: upgraded
`cas-forex\cas_forex\movement_stream.py` and `/api/forex/movement/snapshot`
so the movement stream can accept configured-provider `MarketSnapshot` quotes
while retaining deterministic paper-route fallback. The API now supports
`/api/forex/movement/snapshot?use_provider=true`; when requested it fetches
snapshots from the configured `MarketDataConnector` per active paper symbol and
fails soft per symbol if the provider is unavailable. Provider snapshots update
current price, floating P/L, bid/ask/spread, candle high/low/close, session, and
market-open state, but they still do not authorize live orders or money movement.

2026-06-22 CAS Forex priority synthesis: user designated CAS Forex as the urgent near-term focus because personal/project runway is tight, and asked to pull in CAS_PCBA_Engineer, CAS Harness, and TKS_FORMAL_MANUAL_v6.1_CLEAN as strengthening context. Created `docs\working\cas_forex_priority_synthesis_20260622.md`. Current verdict: CAS Forex has a strong audited research/backtest chassis, but the trading edge remains `OPEN` and live trading remains `NOT LICENSED`. Real-data FX-major results remain narrow and negative after realistic costs: SMA and Chronos lose materially; mean-reversion with trend filter is only a thin in-sample positive and flips negative around normal retail all-in costs; regime switching works end-to-end but does not beat the best single FX-major strategy. Recommended next move is not live money: implement non-FX instrument/point-value support for NAS100/US30/XAU, add broker-grade data/cost realism, run index/gold trend/breakout plus owner 2020 rules, then add walk-forward validation and real 30-trading-day paper proof. CAS_PCBA_Engineer should contribute CCDL board specs, TP1-TP5 trade test points, CSB/CDAB/CMB/CPMB/COB diagnostics, fault-injection tests, and circuit registry/rollback discipline, but must not create broker `ACCEPT`. CAS Harness should contribute Evidence Packet, proof UI, `/verify`-style replay, and claim-status discipline. TKS v6.1 should contribute RPM prerequisite/failure propagation, fractal stability, and type/effect boundaries so research evidence is not confused with live permission.

2026-06-22 CAS Forex walk-forward RPM update: added `cas-forex\cas_forex\walk_forward.py` and `cas-forex\scripts\run_walk_forward_condition_lab.py` so Condition Lab route maps are trained on one historical window, frozen, and evaluated only on the next untouched future window. The evaluator uses prior training bars as signal context without scoring training-window P&L, preventing long-lookback settings from being falsely inert in shorter test windows. First corrected D1 Yahoo-proxy run across `NAS100`, `US30`, `EUR/USD`, `GBP/USD`, and `AUD/USD` with `tsmom_252_500_2_8` plus `range_scalper_24_120` produced `18` windows, `7` routed windows, `3` active routed windows, `3 / 3` positive active routed windows, routed total return-percent sum `3.8480%`, active routed mean return `1.2827%`, routed max drawdown `2.2980%`, and `32` routed trades; research grade remains `NOT_PROMOTED` because route coverage is sparse and positive routed window rate is only `0.4286`. Next gate: build a more unsupported-balanced outcome-native bank with at least `80` unsupported outputs, `40` mixed exact-prompt groups, and at least `8` unsupported rows per topic, then rerun W1/W8/W16 and require hidden-state BA `>= 0.75` plus `>= 0.15` margin over prompt text, char text, layer-0, and generated-prefix controls before any claim upgrade.

## 2026-06-12 v6 Matched-Prompt Chain-Break Tooling

Implemented:

- `live-trace-worker/scripts/build_matched_prompt_chain_break_bank.py` builds exact repeated prompt groups for W0 chain-break campaigns. The default `borderline` bank has `40` exact prompt groups and `640` rows. The `calibrated` variant has `80` exact prompt groups and `640` rows split between borderline and anchored-safe prompts.

- `live-trace-worker/scripts/evaluate_behavior_v6_matched_prespeech.py` separates prompt-level W0 chain-break risk from sample-specific stochastic branch prediction. ## 2026-06-11 Enabled-Predicate Probe Upgrade From Chapter 29 Review

Codex reviewed the attached Chapter-29 coalgebra proposal against the already-shipped quarantine/v5 tooling. Decision: the proposal is partially superior and has been applied where it is stronger.

Accepted and implemented:

- The W0 pre-speech target is now framed as a coalgebraic enabled-operator transition predicate, not a static CO-k state classifier.
- `COMPLIED` maps to `enables_2` / `CO-2`.
- `REFUSED` maps to `enables_3` / `CO-3`.
- `live-trace-worker/scripts/evaluate_behavior_v5_prespeech.py` now reports `target_semantics`, `enabled_predicate_distribution`, and an explicit boundary that TKS licenses the question but does not itself prove the transformer hidden-state claim.
- The v5 hidden and text readouts now use the original CAS probe method contract: `StandardScaler + LogisticRegression`, `class_weight: balanced`, `solver: lbfgs`, default `C=0.25`.

Rejected as overclaim:

- The formal coalgebra definitions do not by themselves prove that Qwen or any other transformer linearly exposes `enabled(x)` in W0 hidden states.
- The Next modality does not remove the need for same-payload balance, grouped folds, text/payload/domain/length baselines, restricted permutation, shuffled-label controls, and live steering/ablation.
- Therefore pre-speech/live interception, causal compliance-state detection, payload-independent comply/refuse detection, prompt-injection prevention/removal/reversal, and hallucination prevention remain `OPEN` and `NOT LICENSED` until the empirical gates pass.

## 2026-06-11 Pre-Speech / Causal Solution Round Table

Codex ran a four-agent local-docs round table over TKS v7.4, Noetic Topos, current Qwen probe diagnostics, and product-claim boundaries for the user's requested flagship question: can CAS Harness catch comply/refuse state before the model speaks and prove that state causes the output? The resulting design brief is `docs/working/cas_harness_prespeech_topos_causal_solution_roundtable_20260611.md`.

Key conclusion: the foundations point to a working two-lane solution, but not to an already-proven hidden-state claim.

- Product lane now: build CAS Harness Instruction Integrity Review around a Topos Integrity Gate. Treat hallucination and prompt-injection risk as local-to-global failures: evidence, task, policy hierarchy, provenance, D/W/P prerequisites, admissibility, and generated claim/action must glue before trust. Missing coverage, task replacement, policy override, lacunarity, or leakage should route to `FLAG_FOR_HUMAN_REVIEW` in shadow mode.
- Research lane next: run a v5 Pre-Realization Refusal Recipe Probe. Do not probe a crude global `COMPLIED` / `REFUSED` bit. Estimate a refusal-readiness recipe using `^3^P|T`, `^3^P|A`, `^3^P|f`, `^8^P|T`, polarity margin, D/W/P prerequisites, coverage/gluing, lacunarity, leakage, and limiting-reagent features.
- Causal lane next: prove behavior change by live prompt-final W0 steering on heldout prompts, with paired seeds, no-op, `+refusal`, `-refusal`, random same-norm control, projection ablation, cross-family judgment, and prompt-level statistics.

The current Qwen diagnostic remains blocked for public pre-speech or causal claims: global W0 hidden readout looked strong, but text-hash baseline beat it; the within-payload gate was insufficient; v4 matched prompts had only `G_matched=2` versus the needed `G_matched>=40`. The sharper data blocker is balanced `COMPLIED` and `REFUSED` examples inside the same payload classes, not just more refusals.

Claim boundary remains strict: pre-speech/live interception, causal compliance-state detection, payload-independent comply/refuse detection, prompt-injection prevention/removal/reversal, harmful-instruction repair, and hallucination prevention remain `OPEN` and `NOT LICENSED`. The marketable slice is shadow Instruction Integrity Review with Evidence Packet persistence and `FLAG_FOR_HUMAN_REVIEW` routing until hosted proof, external/red-team benchmarks, WORM-backed verifier policy, and v5 probe/intervention gates pass.

Recommended next move: build the hosted Instruction Integrity Review card in the deployed CAS Harness path, then implement v5 tooling in the beta-candidate branch: balanced same-payload prompt-bank screener, group-aware W0 analyzer with text/payload/domain baselines, Qwen live steering capture, paired causal statistics, and blinded judge merge with degeneration checks.

## 2026-06-10 Foundational Topos / Instruction Integrity Round Table

Codex ran a four-lane agent round table over local TKS v7.4, Noetic Topos, existing code hooks, and product-security claim boundaries. The resulting design brief is `docs/working/cas_harness_topos_instruction_integrity_roundtable_20260610.md`.

Key product direction: build a CAS Harness "Instruction Integrity" lane around a Topos Integrity Gate. Treat hallucination and prompt injection as local-to-global failures: a generated claim, instruction, tool call, or answer segment must glue back to source evidence, task intent, policy hierarchy, D/W/P prerequisites, admissible transitions, and Evidence Packet provenance. Missing support, task drift, policy override, or lacunarity spikes should route to `UNDETERMINED`, `SUPPRESS`, or `FLAG_FOR_HUMAN_REVIEW`, not customer-facing `ACCEPT`.

Reusable local foundations found:

- TKS v7.4: evidence spans, confidence, "no hidden semantic jumps," lacunarity as gaps/contradictions/missing D/W/P links, and CAS-as-source-of-truth claim discipline.
- Noetic Topos: coverage, gluing, pullback stability, domain precedence (`P > C > E > O`), admissibility guards, and 3-valued `Valid` / `Invalid` / `Undetermined` logic.
- Existing code: RPM gate, domain coherence, inversion scan, refusal/social-engineering heuristics, public explainer firewall, partial CAS inversion, Evidence Packet verifier gates, and WORM-backed verdict discipline.

Claim boundary remains strict: prompt-injection detection/prevention and hallucination prevention are `OPEN` and `NOT LICENSED` as public claims. Current evidence supports a design/prototype direction and shadow-mode flags, not a production hijack detector. The Qwen injection spike remains behavioral-divergence evidence only; typed-probe v4 still must prove clean/refused/complied separation with grouped prompt folds before any internal-state hijack claim.

Implementation status is tracked in the Instruction Integrity stage sections below. Initial enforcement must remain limited to shadow `FLAG_FOR_HUMAN_REVIEW` until hosted proof, external/red-team benchmarks, thresholds, and WORM-backed verifier policy exist. CAS-owned AI surfaces such as the Guide and Evidence Packet explainer can later quarantine detected untrusted spans from active context while preserving raw spans in the packet.

Codex wired the Path-B comply/refuse detection handoff into CAS Harness as a claim-honest packet lane on branch `codex/cas-harness-public-beta-candidate`. This is not a live-interception or causal claim.

## 1:1 Metaphor Map

| Technical thing | Plain-English meaning | Metaphor | Exact mapping |
|---|---|---|---|
| CAS Harness | The audit product | Inspection station | Developers bring a model; CAS Harness inspects it. |
| Live worker | Machine that runs the model | Factory machine | It performs the actual model run. |
| Evidence Packet | Saved audit proof | Inspection report | It records what was tested and proven. |
| WORM anchor | Immutable saved record | Locked filing cabinet | It prevents the report from being silently changed. |
| Matched controls | Fair comparison tests | Control lane | It proves the result is special, not just ordinary behavior. |
| Fleet Executor | Distributed audit orchestrator for very large models | Air traffic control tower | It assigns workers, tracks shards, verifies fragments, and merges one final packet. |
| Production | Public reliable service | Storefront | Users can pay, run audits, and trust the result. |
| CRM | Customer relationship management system | Sales command center | It tracks prospects, investors, pilots, follow-ups, and deal stages. |
| GDPR/CCPA/SOC 2 | Compliance obligations | Building code and inspection | These define the legal/security rules the product must satisfy before serious customers trust it. |

## Product Vision

CAS Harness should become a platform where any developer can audit open-weight models up to 4 trillion parameters, with the claim ladder kept honest:

- `<=20B`: empirically validated lane.
- `20B-300B`: engineered but not size-validated lane.
- `300B-4T`: `EXPERIMENTAL_NOT_VALIDATED` frontier lane behind explicit gates.
- `>4T`: refused.

The completed public product should let a user:

1. Create an account.
2. Choose an audit target.
3. Connect a hosted open-weight model, customer endpoint, or reserved GPU job.
4. Run the audit.
5. Receive an Evidence Packet.
6. Verify WORM anchoring.
7. Understand the claim status without reading methodology notes.
8. Share or export the packet for investors, customers, regulators, or internal review.

Do not use that archived MTC result as Pythia uniqueness proof.

Do not use this as Pythia uniqueness proof or a Pythia `ACCEPT`.

It did not run the TKS 28 Foundations axis, and the report's `condition3_nc28_topic_function` taxonomy is a non-CAS control (`topic_7 x function_4`), not the TKS 28 Foundations.

It has 816 rows: 40E TKS `A1-D10` classes and F28 TKS `1a-7d` classes, 12 rows per class, 8/4 train/test split. Regression test `tests\test_definition_locked_tks_fixture.py` prevents fallback to CAS translation-key labels such as `^1|P` or `F1|T`.

The definition-locked TKS40/F28 fixture is the grounding lane: it can show GPT-NeoX-20B can recover the owner TKS axes from the locked definitions, but it cannot close uniqueness because the prompts intentionally contain TKS titles/source definitions. The Condition-3 blind fixture is the uniqueness lane: it remains the fair matched-control test for CAS specialness, and any claim-bearing result must carry the locked owner TKS `definition_sha256`. The replacement H200 pod at `212.247.220.166:18611` timed out during the next-run check, so no new GPU capture was started in this session.

Customer audit creation now refuses with HTTP 503 unless the packaged app definition file `api\resources\tks_owner_locked_definitions_20260603.json` and active probe-bundle manifest `api\resources\tks_definition_probe_bundle_manifest.v1.json` both match locked hash `a124302ed93c7b8571b24a5cdf14433adf904eb94fd65895c6fd25fae58e28a0`. Frontier/customer-supplied probe specs are also locked: `api\services\cas_harness_probe_compatibility.py` marks a probe `PROBE_COMPATIBILITY_UNSUPPORTED` unless its `definition_sha256` equals that same owner TKS hash, and the public distributed route returns HTTP 422. Current manifest stays honest: `definition_fixture_locked_no_trained_probe_claim`, not a trained-probe claim.
  - 2026-06-03 comparison-boundary note: the TKS definition lock applies to the claim-bearing CAS audit/probe lane. It must not be interpreted as "never run non-CAS comparisons." Users still need matched-control and non-CAS framework comparison lanes to test CAS specialness, but those lanes must be explicitly labeled as `control` / `comparator` evidence and must never be accepted as the primary CAS probe or used to emit `ACCEPT` unless the CAS lane itself is locked to the owner TKS definitions and all other proof gates pass. `live-trace-worker\scripts\build_stage9_40e_verifier_report.py` now enforces this for Stage 9 40E: a passing matched-taxonomy/uniqueness proof cannot close the uniqueness gate unless it carries the locked owner TKS `definition_sha256`; unbound comparator evidence stays unavailable for promotion, while explicit failures can still suppress.
  - 2026-06-03 launch-framing note: uniqueness/MTC is a research-verdict gate for future `ACCEPT`, not a public-product launch gate. CAS Harness can launch as an honest audit harness that emits immutable, scoped evidence and `FLAG_FOR_HUMAN_REVIEW` when gates remain open. Do not block public rollout on turning the Pythia packet into `ACCEPT`; block it on product readiness instead: user access, billing/quotas, worker route without SSH tunnel, UI clarity, compliance minimum, and truthful durability/retention claims.

## Path To Public Production

### 1. Kill The SSH Tunnel

Goal: deploy the live worker behind a real production route.

Tasks:

- [ ] Deploy the live worker with a real public/private production endpoint.
- [ ] Require token auth or stronger service-to-service authentication.
- [ ] Add health checks.
- [ ] Add restart recovery.
- [ ] Confirm no laptop or SSH tunnel is needed for normal operation.
- [ ] Record worker version, git SHA, or deployed code hash in Evidence Packets.

Plain-English reason: the product cannot depend on someone's laptop staying connected.

### 2. Run Old Strong Fixtures Through The New Live Product Path

Goal: connect the old research strength to the new product pipeline.

Tasks:

- [ ] Run saved Pythia/TKS high-signal fixtures through the live Evidence Packet flow.
- [ ] Preserve exact fixture labels and method labels.
- [ ] Compare new packet outputs against saved historical results.
- [ ] Do not replace old results unless the new run is method-equivalent.
- [ ] Write a bridge note explaining old research result versus new product result.

Known historical results to preserve:

- Pythia-160M 28 Foundations: 78.6%.
- Pythia-160M 40 Elements: 89.8%.
- Foundation subspace ablation: 78.6% to 3.57% chance.
- GPT-NeoX-20B Stage 9 40E: 95.625% hidden readout versus about 49.24% text baseline.
- GPT-NeoX-20B saved local validation artifacts are not `GREY`: `D:\tksCAS-archive\gpt_neox_20b_stage9_original_40e_closure_20260527\gpt_neox_20b_stage9_original_40e_probe_eval_report.json` records hidden readout `test_acc: 0.95625`; `D:\tksCAS-archive\gpt_neox_20b_stage9_original_40e_bridge_proof_20260528\gpt_neox_20b_stage9_original_40e_eta_epsilon_bridge_per2_20260528T0455Z.json` records `PROBE_PREDICTED_40E_ETA_EPSILON_BRIDGE_PASS` with selected-element accuracy `0.975`; the same bridge packet remains `FLAG_FOR_HUMAN_REVIEW` only because the verifier tier is missing. The separate `GREY` result applies to the later GPT-NeoX-20B Condition-3 blind matched-taxonomy comparator, not to these saved Stage 9 original 40E PASS/readout artifacts.

Plain-English reason: old scores are the lab exam; the new product path is the checkout counter. We need the lab exam to pass through the checkout counter without losing truth.

### 3. Add Matched Controls

Goal: turn "a causal effect happened" into "the causal effect beats fair comparison tests."

Tasks:

- [ ] Add matched random/control ablations.
- [ ] Keep same layer/rank/target budget across real and control runs.
- [ ] Save control results into the Evidence Packet.
- [ ] Flip `control_proof` to `present` only when matched controls actually run.
- [ ] Keep `control_proof: unavailable` when controls are absent.

Plain-English reason: flipping one breaker proves something changed. Matched controls prove it changed for the right reason.

### 4. Lock The Public Claim Ladder

Goal: users must understand what the audit does and does not prove.

The UI and packet must clearly separate:

- [ ] Live trace.
- [ ] Readout proof.
- [ ] Causal proof.
- [ ] Matched-control proof.
- [ ] WORM-anchored packet.
- [ ] `ACCEPT` versus `FLAG_FOR_HUMAN_REVIEW`.
- [ ] `LICENSED` versus `NOT LICENSED` claims.

Plain-English reason: do not let a customer mistake a partial proof for a full verdict.

### 5. Declutter The UI

Goal: make the product usable by a normal developer or buyer.

Owner UI direction (2026-06-04): the CAS Harness launch UI should move toward a clean, minimal, highly functional workspace inspired by ChatGPT, Codex, and Claude Code rather than a dense research dashboard. Preserve the existing CAS/Noetic color language and the ten-dot Tree-of-Life mark from `https://noeticcompass.com/compiler`, including the subtle opacity animation moving through the dot formation. The primary product surface should feel like an audit command center: a clear input/composer area, a left rail or compact history for audit runs, a central run transcript/timeline, and a right-side evidence/status panel. Users must be able to see that an audit has started, watch it progress through visible stages, understand what is running now, see completion/failure, and open the final Evidence Packet/verdict without reading raw JSON.

Launch flow:

- [ ] Choose model.
- [ ] Connect key or hosted worker.
- [ ] Run audit.
- [ ] See result.
- [ ] Download/share packet.
- [ ] Open FAQ/tooltips for methodology details.

Move most methodology text into:

- [ ] FAQ.
- [ ] Tooltips.
- [ ] Expandable "technical details" panels.
- [ ] Exported methodology documents.

Plain-English reason: the first screen should help users complete the audit, not make them read the whole research history.

### 6. Add Accounts, Billing, And Quotas

Goal: make the product usable as a business.

Tasks:

- [ ] Add user accounts.
- [ ] Add organization/workspace support.
- [ ] Add free tier.
- [ ] Add paid live-audit tier.
- [ ] Add enterprise/reserved GPU tier.
- [ ] Add usage quotas. PARTIAL: Agent 5 wired the default-off server quota gate on `launch/paid-quotas`; still needs commit/merge/deploy/hosted smoke with account mode enabled.
- [ ] Add billing provider.
- [ ] Add invoice/receipt flow.
- [ ] Add admin dashboard.

Suggested tiering:

- Free: demos/reference packets.
- Paid: live audits on supported models.
- Enterprise: larger open-weight models and reserved GPU jobs.

### 7. Scale Model Support

Goal: support the "audit open-weight models up to 4T parameters" vision while keeping 300B-4T labeled `EXPERIMENTAL_NOT_VALIDATED` until real runs complete.

Model lanes:

- [ ] Small lane: Pythia/1B/7B, cheap live audits.
- [ ] Medium lane: 20B/70B, queued jobs.
- [ ] Frontier lane: 300B to 4T, reserved cluster jobs, `EXPERIMENTAL_NOT_VALIDATED`.

Rules:

- [ ] Same packet schema across lanes.
- [ ] Different compute backend by size.
- [ ] Fleet Executor required for 100B+ and frontier-scale lanes.
- [ ] Each worker runs a sub-harness on its assigned layer/model shard.
- [ ] Every worker emits fragment hashes, proof chips, and worker summaries.
- [ ] Fleet aggregator verifies fragment hashes, stitches the multi-layer trace, reconciles shard seams, and emits one merged Evidence Packet.
- [ ] Conservative fleet rule: any missing/invalid worker fragment prevents fleet `ACCEPT`.
- [x] Always require model provenance for frontier runs.
- [x] Always require declared checkpoint hashes/revision for frontier runs.
- [x] Always distinguish original publisher from converted checkpoint provider in the provenance manifest.

Large-model audit components that must not be omitted:

- [ ] Scale Planner: computes GPU count, memory budget, precision, shard plan, runtime estimate, storage estimate, and cost estimate before launch.
- [x] Model/weight manifest gate: frontier runs require model identity, revision/checkpoint, license tag, and SHA256 declarations before creation.
- [x] Architecture adapter gate: frontier runs require a declared architecture that maps to supported hook families before creation.
- [x] Probe compatibility gate: frontier runs require a loadable `cas_probe_linear_v1` probe declaration before creation.
- [ ] Artifact object store: stores large activation/logit/manifold artifacts outside the packet, with URI, byte size, SHA256, layer range, sample range, and worker ID.
- [x] State substrate contract: tenant-scoped records, optimistic versioning, leases, traceability envelope, conformance tests, and concurrency stress harness exist.
- [x] Doc 10 Phase 0/1/2 migration seam: live globals can dual-write to `StateStore` and shadow-read with zero divergence while remaining dark by default.
- [ ] Queue/resume/idempotency: every large audit must be restartable by `trace_id`, shard ID, and stage without losing completed work; operational ledger-read enable, Phase 4 dict deletion, and multi-host durable state remain open.
- [ ] Observability: live status for queued/running/failed/retried/completed shards, GPU health, memory pressure, throughput, and ETA.
- [x] State-ledger rollout observability: `GET /api/state-ledger/status` reports dark/enabled/read-authoritative posture and divergence tally for the ledger migration.
- [x] Cost and authorization gate: frontier-scale runs require explicit customer acknowledgment before creation.
- [ ] Security isolation: customer data, model weights, activations, credentials, and artifacts must be tenant-isolated.
- [ ] Failure taxonomy: distinguish model-load failure, shard failure, probe failure, causal failure, control failure, merge failure, WORM failure, and verifier failure.
- [x] Experimental label gate: 300B-4T lanes stay `EXPERIMENTAL_NOT_VALIDATED` until real runs complete.
- [x] HTTP experimental-risk acknowledgement gate: 300B-4T plans require `accept_experimental_frontier=true` and return `EXPERIMENTAL_NOT_VALIDATED`.
- [x] Open-weight access gate: distributed audits refuse non-open-weight/text-only modes at the service choke point.
- [x] Data-capture acknowledgment gate: distributed audits refuse creation unless the caller acknowledges audit capture, write-once retention, and CAS-Native reuse.

### 8. Launch In Phases

Launch sequence:

- [ ] Internal production smoke.
- [ ] Private alpha with trusted developers.
- [ ] Paid NDA beta.
- [ ] Public launch.

Scoped-beta roadmap proposal from the 2026-06-03 launch swarm, pending owner execution approval:

## Compliance TODO

Assume we are not GDPR/CCPA/SOC 2 production-ready until these are complete.

### Launch-Compliance Minimum

- [ ] Privacy Policy.
- [ ] Terms of Service.
- [ ] Data Processing Addendum (DPA).
- [ ] Subprocessor list.
- [ ] Data retention policy.
- [ ] DSAR workflow: access, correction, deletion, export, opt-out where applicable.
- [ ] Security policy.
- [ ] Incident response plan.
- [ ] Vendor risk register.

### SOC 2 Readiness

- [ ] SOC 2 control matrix.
- [ ] Access-control policy.
- [ ] MFA requirement for production systems.
- [ ] Production access log.
- [ ] Periodic access review.
- [ ] Change-management process.
- [ ] Incident-response test.
- [ ] Vendor review records.
- [ ] Security training record.
- [ ] Monitoring and alerting evidence.

### CAS-Specific Compliance Risk

Treat every audit run as potentially sensitive customer data unless proven otherwise.

Audit runs may include:

- Prompts.
- Outputs.
- Activations.
- Audit metadata.
- Evidence Packets.
- Model configuration.
- Customer endpoint details.
- Customer API/provider information.

WORM/Object Lock needs special handling because deletion rights and immutable evidence can conflict. The retention policy must clearly define what is immutable audit evidence, how long it is retained, and what lawful basis applies.

## CRM TODO

A CRM is required before public launch.

Plain-English meaning: the CRM is the business memory system. It tracks every customer, investor, partner, lead, conversation, follow-up, NDA, demo, and deal stage.

Recommended start:

- [ ] Use HubSpot Free, Airtable, Notion database, or Google Sheet immediately.
- [ ] Do not start with Salesforce unless enterprise sales becomes complex.

Minimum CRM fields:

- [ ] Company.
- [ ] Person.
- [ ] Role/title.
- [ ] Email.
- [ ] Category: investor, buyer, researcher, partner, developer, compliance buyer.
- [ ] Status: new, contacted, NDA sent, demo scheduled, packet sent, follow-up, closed.
  - 2026-06-03 note: first-rung `pipeline_status` exists in production for `new`, `contacted`, `qualified`, `won`, and `lost`; the richer NDA/demo/packet/follow-up workflow remains open.
- [ ] Last contact date.
- [ ] Next action date.
- [ ] Notes.
- [ ] Risk level.
- [ ] Interest level.
- [ ] Requested materials.
- [ ] NDA signed: yes/no.
- [ ] Deal value estimate.
- [ ] Objections/questions.

## Post-Launch AI Agent Service TODO

This is intentionally deferred until after the core CAS Harness product is launched or launch-ready.

Goal: build a 24/7 AI agent service, in the style of `Clawdbot`, `Hermes`, and `Moltbot`, that helps with customer service, marketing support, and site maintenance.

Bounded explainer note: the local Gemini CAS Harness "Guide" page is a public-knowledge-base assistant only. It may explain the user workflow and safe status language, but it cannot take actions, edit content, make marketing claims, or reveal proprietary implementation details.

Required boundaries before implementation:

- [ ] Define which actions agents may take directly versus which require human review.
- [ ] Define customer-support claim rules so agents cannot fabricate `ACCEPT`, `LICENSED`, `PROVENANCE_VERIFIED`, WORM anchoring, pricing, legal, or compliance claims.
- [ ] Define marketing approval gates so generated copy cannot overstate CAS Harness validation results.
- [ ] Define site-maintenance permissions, rollback rules, and audit logging before any agent can edit live content.
- [ ] Implement an agent authority/claim-governance layer before public autonomous operation.
- [ ] Keep irreversible actions human-gated until the governance layer is tested.

## Recommended Immediate Next Move

The immediate production move is human-owned infrastructure and business/legal sign-off:

1. Confirm the USPTO/legal/IP posture before public claim language goes live.
2. Approve customer-data consent/licensing terms because CAS Harness captures model I/O and audit traces.
3. Choose the production route for the live worker, accounts, billing, quotas, and public access.
4. Lock the UI claim ladder so public wording stays scoped validation, not customer `ACCEPT`.
5. Continue matched-control work so `control_proof` can eventually move from candidate-only / human-review to verified evidence.

Optional engineering follow-ups:

- Keep `NC_STATE_LEDGER_READ_AUTHORITY=true` monitored on hosted Render; if any read-authority issue appears, rollback is to set `NC_STATE_LEDGER_READ_AUTHORITY=false` and re-run the shadow verifier.
- Keep hosted paid-disk durability monitored: current proof is single-host/single-instance restart survival on Render disk `noetic-data`; host-loss and multi-instance durability still require managed Postgres or equivalent replication.
- Activate the managed-Postgres path only after a real DSN is configured: set `NC_DATABASE_URL`, redeploy, run the hosted state-ledger verifier, and keep the claim scoped until multi-instance/failover behavior is also proven.
- Do not treat read-authority proof as permission for Phase 4 deletion; `_FRAGMENT_REFS` replay guard must be re-pointed first and doc 13's deletion plan must be executed deliberately.
- Keep the live-Postgres claim scoped: `PostgresStateStore` contract/restart/fencing is verified on one live server; host-loss survival still needs synchronous replication and failover testing.
- Do not delete `_DISTRIBUTED_AUDITS`, `_WORKERS`, `_FLEET_RUNS`, or `_FRAGMENT_REFS` until read-authority cutover is proven and doc 10 Phase 4 grep-clean is ready.
- Extend the state substrate toward doc 11 multi-host production: Postgres/Redis backend, fencing tokens, and clear truthful claims for durability and lease safety.
- Make Fleet Executor the required orchestration layer for 100B+ / 300B-4T experimental model lanes.
- Add worker-side verification upgrades for `PROVENANCE_VERIFIED`, `ARCHITECTURE_ADAPTER_VERIFIED`, `PROBE_COMPATIBILITY_VERIFIED`, `COMPUTE_FEASIBILITY_VERIFIED`, `COST_METERED`, and `DATA_CAPTURE_WORM_COMMITTED`.
- Add queue-based async anchoring for high-volume or large-model runs.
- Unify packet discovery across non-Stage-9 lanes such as S7/F28 and live customer audit packets.
- Add explicit object-store wiring for >20B activation/manifold artifacts.
- Run the 1.5B forward-pass cover-ablation cleanup if needed.
- Continue matched-control work so `control_proof` can eventually move from `unavailable` to `present`.
- Promote Instruction Integrity beyond shadow mode only after hosted authenticated proof, expanded external/red-team clean/injected/control benchmarks, and verifier policy are proven. Local Evidence Packet persistence now exists, and the local v0 benchmark gate passes, but status remains shadow-only: it may show `FLAG_FOR_HUMAN_REVIEW` risk findings, but it must not be marketed as hallucination prevention, prompt-injection removal, harmful-instruction inversion, or automatic `ACCEPT`/`SUPPRESS` enforcement.

Clean public story to earn:

> This is not a demo. CAS Harness runs a real open-weight model, captures live activations, performs causal intervention, compares against controls, and writes a WORM-anchored Evidence Packet.

- 2026-06-04: token-security note from production closeout. The dashboard stores the scoped-beta JWT in browser `localStorage` as `nc_jwt_token`, so a logged-in user can copy their own bearer token from DevTools. This is acceptable for internal smoke testing with short-lived tokens, but not launch-grade paid auth. Before paid/external users, replace the shared mint-secret flow with real accounts or invite-issued credentials, keep quotas/paywall/tenant/role decisions on the server, and restrict privileged ops endpoints such as evidence anchoring away from ordinary customer tokens.
